Patch 6.0.10¶

What changed in the game data from 6.0.9 to 6.0.10, found by comparing the two versions. Struck text is gone, marked text is new. Only the data is compared: a change to how the code works doesn't show here.

Crafting¶

Changed (18)¶

Name Type Change
Keen Strike ×2, Steady Pick ×2, Ore Hauling ×2, Weak spot rate ×2, Weak spot duration, Weak spot size, Digging area, Unusual ore, Chance to Shatter ore on Mining, Mining area, Extra ore on mine, Mining damage, Fault Lines, Keen Eye Craft notable Ore Portals: Ore and metal bars pass through portals once the boss of their biome is dead; the portal keeps a share of what passes (25%).; • Copper, tin, bronze — The Elder; • Iron — Bonemass; • Silver — Moder; • Black metal — Yagluth; • Flametal — Fader; • Runic ores and their alloys — the boss of the biome where their vein is found

Keywords¶

Changed (6)¶

Name Change
Attributes Strength supports → raises health regeneration and carry weight; Dexterity supports → raises stamina regeneration and its → evasion regeneration → rating; Intelligence supports → raises eitr regeneration and rune shield. Governing attributes also raise weapon damage through the skill multiplier.
Eitr The resource spent on magic. Food and Intelligence can increase → raises its maximum; Intelligence speeds up its regeneration.
Ore Portals Allows ore and metal bars, runic ores and their alloys included, through portals after the required boss is defeated. The portal consumes a configured share of the transported metal.
Ore through portals Allows ore and metal bars, runic ores and their alloys included, through portals after the required boss is defeated. The portal consumes the configured share of transported metal; each metal follows its own progression requirement.
Potential An item's budget for runic crafting: 100 → 65 on every ordinary piece of equipment. Each runic action costs 15 plus the costs of its glyphs (alloys and → cost glyphs → no potential); a critical craft costs half. At zero the item still works, but runic crafting is closed. Repairs and workbench upgrades do not restore it. Base jewels have potential too. The property «increased item potential» raises the maximum; the item remembers what it has spent, so a reroll gives potential back only by rolling higher than the value it was spent under.
Stamina The resource spent on movement, attacks, blocking and tool work. Dexterity → Food can improve → raises its maximum; and → Dexterity speeds up its regeneration.
